This dirties the submodule working tree on every boot. The drift CI workflow MUST exempt wgp_config.json from its diff check or it will fire false-positive drift alerts on every run. Use one of: (a) 'git diff --ignore-submodules=dirty', (b) filter out wgp_config.json in the drift comparison, or (c) git ls-files -m excluding that path. Confirm the workflow output is green against a booted worker state before calling the drift CI deliverable complete.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance for Sprint 4 of the Wan2GP fork migration. This is iteration-4-approved work with seven flags verified and two accepted-tradeoff flags (both the same root cause: .venv lacks pip/pyyaml \u2014 executor uses the regex-tokenizer yaml check as the primary path, orchestrator runs full yaml.safe_load on the GitHub runner).\n\nCritical correctness focus (iter-3 cluster): the apply_runtime_model_definition_patch guard MUST be dual-target \u2014 it snapshots BOTH runtime.models_def[model_name] AND (when transformer_type == model_name and wan_model is not None) runtime.wan_model.model_def, matching _RuntimePatchTransaction.__init__ at source/runtime/wgp_ports/runtime_registry.py:189-192. Guard A short-circuits only when registry + BOTH live targets match; Guard B on drift of EITHER target calls rollback_wgp_patches then a fresh transaction. Tests T3.2/T3.3/T3.4 pin exactly this behavior; do not simplify them to probe only models_def.\n\nScope discipline (iter-3 tiebreaker option C): Sprint 4 uses a single default context_id in the production apply_all_wgp_patches call. Multi-context concurrent runtime-patch isolation against a shared WGP runtime is EXPLICITLY DEFERRED to Sprint 5. Do NOT add a same-model cross-context test \u2014 it would either pass while semantics remain broken or fail on correct code, both bad. The bootstrap-records test (T3.5) uses context_id='test.bootstrap' \u2014 this is the only non-default context coverage Sprint 4 needs. The runbook Sprint-5 followup section must include a concrete reproducer outline so Sprint 5 has an obvious starting point.\n\nEnvironment gotchas: (1) Bare `python` is 3.8.10 in this workspace and CANNOT parse source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py's 3.10 annotations \u2014 every smoke invocation pins `.venv/bin/python` or `uv run --python 3.10`. (2) `uv sync` fails on darwin-arm64 because the decord==0.6.0 wheel is missing; fall back to `.venv/bin/python -m pytest <files>`. (3) `.venv` has no pip module \u2014 no pyyaml install is possible; the regex-tokenizer yaml check (asserting `on:`, `workflow_dispatch`, `schedule`, `cron`) is the gate. (4) scripts/sprint4/patch_lifecycle_smoke.py is orchestrator/linux-only \u2014 do NOT attempt to run it on darwin-arm64.\n\nExecutor sandbox rules (learned from Sprints 1-3): no writes under .git/, no push to remotes, no `gh pr create`. Orchestrator owns all commit/tag/push/PR operations. Leave changes `git add`-staged but uncommitted. Baseline tag wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline already exists \u2014 don't recreate it.\n\nPR draft derivation is literal: 14 files, one per hunk string from `grep ',upstreamable,' docs/wan2gp-triage.csv | cut -d, -f2 | sort -u`. Affected-files lists are also grep-derived. No invented groupings.\n\nExecution order per the approved plan: T1 (audit) \u2192 T2 (wrapper + wiring) \u2192 T3 (5 tests). T4, T5, T6, T7 can proceed in parallel after T2 (T4/T5/T6 only truly need T1). T8 (handoff note) collects everything; T9 runs all gates and stages. If T9's focused patch tests fail, fix in source/ or tests/ and re-run \u2014 do not create new test files beyond the five named in T3, and do not skip hooks. Write a tiny throwaway reproducer script as part of T9's gate (import apply_all_wgp_patches with a stubbed runtime, print get_wgp_patch_state() showing the ltx2 markers), run it to confirm, then delete it.\n\nDebt avoidance: do not bootstrap pip into .venv as a workaround for the yaml check \u2014 the tokenizer approach is the principled path in this environment and is already the accepted gate tradeoff.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared the proposed worker sigterm handling to the existing lifecycle structure in source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py:529-579. the queue entrypoint wraps startup and steady-state in one lifecycle block, but worker `main()` does not enter its cleanup `try/finally` until source/runtime/worker/server.py:405, so the proposed handler alone is not fully correct for startup-time shutdowns. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared the documented startup limitation to the actual lifecycle boundary in source/runtime/worker/server.py. because the cleanup try/finally still begins only at line 405, the proposed sigterm handler is only correct for steady-state shutdowns and not for startup-time sigterm, so the plan remains intentionally incomplete on that case. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] byte-compare-oracle: clear_conditioning oracle is single-shape (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements: did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised supervisor command against the original brief and source/task_handlers/worker/fatal_error_handler.py:443-445. rev 3 no longer claims literal argv equality and instead redefines "byte-identical" as "operator-equivalent", but the user note explicitly asked for byte-identical cli args, env, cwd, and python interpreter, so that requirement is still only partially addressed. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Report-only: only fail on git command error, never on non-zero drift. Dry-run locally: confirm `git -C Wan2GP rev-parse HEAD` equals 181bb71a21008032e4771e11663f33e4489c4512. Validate yaml using the regex-tokenizer fallback as the PRIMARY executor path (per gate tradeoff for FLAG-008/all_locations-v4: .venv has no pip or pyyaml). The tokenizer check must assert the file contains `on:`, `workflow_dispatch`, `schedule`, and `cron`. If `.venv/bin/pip` happens to exist, may additionally run `.venv/bin/pip install --quiet pyyaml && .venv/bin/python -c \"import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml'))\"` but do not make this the gate.   "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance for Sprint 4 of the Wan2GP fork migration. This is iteration-4-approved work with seven flags verified and two accepted-tradeoff flags (both the same root cause: .venv lacks pip/pyyaml \u2014 executor uses the regex-tokenizer yaml check as the primary path, orchestrator runs full yaml.safe_load on the GitHub runner).\n\nCritical correctness focus (iter-3 cluster): the apply_runtime_model_definition_patch guard MUST be dual-target \u2014 it snapshots BOTH runtime.models_def[model_name] AND (when transformer_type == model_name and wan_model is not None) runtime.wan_model.model_def, matching _RuntimePatchTransaction.__init__ at source/runtime/wgp_ports/runtime_registry.py:189-192. Guard A short-circuits only when registry + BOTH live targets match; Guard B on drift of EITHER target calls rollback_wgp_patches then a fresh transaction. Tests T3.2/T3.3/T3.4 pin exactly this behavior; do not simplify them to probe only models_def.\n\nScope discipline (iter-3 tiebreaker option C): Sprint 4 uses a single default context_id in the production apply_all_wgp_patches call. Multi-context concurrent runtime-patch isolation against a shared WGP runtime is EXPLICITLY DEFERRED to Sprint 5. Do NOT add a same-model cross-context test \u2014 it would either pass while semantics remain broken or fail on correct code, both bad. The bootstrap-records test (T3.5) uses context_id='test.bootstrap' \u2014 this is the only non-default context coverage Sprint 4 needs. The runbook Sprint-5 followup section must include a concrete reproducer outline so Sprint 5 has an obvious starting point.\n\nEnvironment gotchas: (1) Bare `python` is 3.8.10 in this workspace and CANNOT parse source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py's 3.10 annotations \u2014 every smoke invocation pins `.venv/bin/python` or `uv run --python 3.10`. (2) `uv sync` fails on darwin-arm64 because the decord==0.6.0 wheel is missing; fall back to `.venv/bin/python -m pytest <files>`. (3) `.venv` has no pip module \u2014 no pyyaml install is possible; the regex-tokenizer yaml check (asserting `on:`, `workflow_dispatch`, `schedule`, `cron`) is the gate. (4) scripts/sprint4/patch_lifecycle_smoke.py is orchestrator/linux-only \u2014 do NOT attempt to run it on darwin-arm64.\n\nExecutor sandbox rules (learned from Sprints 1-3): no writes under .git/, no push to remotes, no `gh pr create`. Orchestrator owns all commit/tag/push/PR operations. Leave changes `git add`-staged but uncommitted. Baseline tag wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline already exists \u2014 don't recreate it.\n\nPR draft derivation is literal: 14 files, one per hunk string from `grep ',upstreamable,' docs/wan2gp-triage.csv | cut -d, -f2 | sort -u`. Affected-files lists are also grep-derived. No invented groupings.\n\nExecution order per the approved plan: T1 (audit) \u2192 T2 (wrapper + wiring) \u2192 T3 (5 tests). T4, T5, T6, T7 can proceed in parallel after T2 (T4/T5/T6 only truly need T1). T8 (handoff note) collects everything; T9 runs all gates and stages. If T9's focused patch tests fail, fix in source/ or tests/ and re-run \u2014 do not create new test files beyond the five named in T3, and do not skip hooks.       "executor_note": "No. The focused patch tests, strict path-contract smoke, tokenizer workflow validation, and throwaway reproducer all ran with the approved `.venv/bin/python` path, the reproducer script was deleted afterward, and the Sprint 4 deliverables are `git add`-staged but uncommitted with no `.git/` writes or push. The remaining gap is that the required 8-file regression suite is not green on a compliant 3.10 executor in this darwin-arm64 workspace because the available `.venv/bin/python` path lacks compatible `numpy`/`cv2`/`PIL` binaries and the reused 3.8 site-packages bridge fails binary imports under 3.10.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance for Sprint 4 of the Wan2GP fork migration. This is iteration-4-approved work with seven flags verified and two accepted-tradeoff flags (both the same root cause: .venv lacks pip/pyyaml \u2014 executor uses the regex-tokenizer yaml check as the primary path, orchestrator runs full yaml.safe_load on the GitHub runner).\n\nCritical correctness focus (iter-3 cluster): the apply_runtime_model_definition_patch guard MUST be dual-target \u2014 it snapshots BOTH runtime.models_def[model_name] AND (when transformer_type == model_name and wan_model is not None) runtime.wan_model.model_def, matching _RuntimePatchTransaction.__init__ at source/runtime/wgp_ports/runtime_registry.py:189-192. Guard A short-circuits only when registry + BOTH live targets match; Guard B on drift of EITHER target calls rollback_wgp_patches then a fresh transaction. Tests T3.2/T3.3/T3.4 pin exactly this behavior; do not simplify them to probe only models_def.\n\nScope discipline (iter-3 tiebreaker option C): Sprint 4 uses a single default context_id in the production apply_all_wgp_patches call. Multi-context concurrent runtime-patch isolation against a shared WGP runtime is EXPLICITLY DEFERRED to Sprint 5. Do NOT add a same-model cross-context test \u2014 it would either pass while semantics remain broken or fail on correct code, both bad. The bootstrap-records test (T3.5) uses context_id='test.bootstrap' \u2014 this is the only non-default context coverage Sprint 4 needs. The runbook Sprint-5 followup section must include a concrete reproducer outline so Sprint 5 has an obvious starting point.\n\nEnvironment gotchas: (1) Bare `python` is 3.8.10 in this workspace and CANNOT parse source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py's 3.10 annotations \u2014 every smoke invocation pins `.venv/bin/python` or `uv run --python 3.10`. (2) `uv sync` fails on darwin-arm64 because the decord==0.6.0 wheel is missing; fall back to `.venv/bin/python -m pytest <files>`. (3) `.venv` has no pip module \u2014 no pyyaml install is possible; the regex-tokenizer yaml check (asserting `on:`, `workflow_dispatch`, `schedule`, `cron`) is the gate. (4) scripts/sprint4/patch_lifecycle_smoke.py is orchestrator/linux-only \u2014 do NOT attempt to run it on darwin-arm64.\n\nExecutor sandbox rules (learned from Sprints 1-3): no writes under .git/, no push to remotes, no `gh pr create`. Orchestrator owns all commit/tag/push/PR operations. Leave changes `git add`-staged but uncommitted. Baseline tag wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline already exists \u2014 don't recreate it.\n\nPR draft derivation is literal: 14 files, one per hunk string from `grep ',upstreamable,' docs/wan2gp-triage.csv | cut -d, -f2 | sort -u`. Affected-files lists are also grep-derived. No invented groupings.\n\nExecution order per the approved plan: T1 (audit) \u2192 T2 (wrapper + wiring) \u2192 T3 (5 tests). T4, T5, T6, T7 can proceed in parallel after T2 (T4/T5/T6 only truly need T1). T8 (handoff note) collects everything; T9 runs all gates and stages. If T9's focused patch tests fail, fix in source/ or tests/ and re-run \u2014 do not create new test files beyond the five named in T3, and do not skip hooks. Write a tiny throwaway reproducer script as part of T9's gate (import apply_all_wgp_patches with a stubbed runtime, print get_wgp_patch_state() showing the ltx2 markers), run it to confirm, then delete it.\n\nDebt avoidance: do not bootstrap pip into .venv as a workaround for the yaml check \u2014 the tokenizer approach is the principled path in this environment and is already the accepted gate tradeoff. Keep the runbook Sprint-5 section concrete so the deferred multi-context work doesn't become permanent debt.",

- If you cannot verify your changes (tests missing or unrunnable), treat this as high risk — re-examine your implementation with extra scrutiny instead of accepting it on faith.
- If tests fail, read the traceback carefully. Diagnose WHY — don't just retry. Common causes: wrong function/method used, missing import, incorrect type, edge case not handled. Fix the root cause, then re-run.
- When verifying changes, run the entire test file or module (e.g., `pytest tests/test_foo.py`), not individual test functions. Individual tests miss regressions in the same module.
- finalize.json includes baseline_test_failures — a list of test IDs that were already failing before your changes. If a test fails and its ID appears in baseline_test_failures, it is pre-existing — do not scope-creep into fixing it. If baseline_test_failures is null, the baseline could not be captured; use your judgment but err on the side of assuming failures are regressions. You MUST still re-run the FULL test suite with your changes applied — pre-existing failures do not excuse skipping verification. Never narrow to individual test functions and stop.
- Before declaring the work complete, write a short script (not a full test) that reproduces the exact bug or incorrect behavior described in the task. Run it to confirm the fix resolves the issue. Then delete the script so it does not appear in the final diff. If the task description is too vague to write a concrete reproduction, note this explicitly in executor_notes.
- Output concrete files changed and commands run. `files_changed` means files you WROTE or MODIFIED — not files you read or verified. Only list files where you made actual edits.
